  	function collapseEmptyAdWrappers(){
  		jQuery('li.flex-item-1-4.vertical-ad').each(function(idx) {
  	        $currLi = jQuery( this );
  			$child = jQuery( this ).children();
  			$adId = $child.attr('id');
  			$adId = $adId.replace('block-dfp-','dfp-ad-');
  			if (!jQuery('#'+$adId).is(':visible')) {
  				$currLi.hide();
  			}
  	    });
  		jQuery('li.flex-item-1-4.horizontal-ad').each(function(idx) {
            $currLi = jQuery( this );

                $currLi.hide();
            }
        });
